More investment because the present and future of the country so demand and because investing today strengthens the economy and employment, when outside the winds blow against it. Better future because it is our responsibility to ensure a decent society and a competitive economy for the present and future generations, because today's acts must not, cannot leave behind our responsibility for the young and the generations that will happen to them. More income, more investment, better future: this is the budget that the country needs. Mr. President, ladies and gentlemen. Members: The State budget effectively reinforces the income of families. Among the reduction in taxes, increases in wages and pensions and increases in social benefits, this budget increases the income of families by EUR 5 billion, permanently and in a single year. [[P.S.'s applause.]] contrary to what the oppositions say, income-enhancing measures far exceed the variation in indirect taxes. The net balance of family income increase measures has little or no precedent. I repeat, EUR 5 billion, i.e. about 500 € per resident. The IRS (tax on income of natural persons) descends into more than EUR 1700 million, by reducing the rates, updating the steps, strengthening the minimum of existence, extending the IRS Young or increasing the deductions of leased expenditure. Increases the national minimum wage to €820, which means more €60 per month for these workers. It's €840 plus per year. [[P.S.'s applause.]] Increases the salary of public servants in at least €52/month, to which the effects of progress are added, at a total increase of around EUR 1500 million. They increase pensions by EUR 2200 million, ensuring the recovery of the purchasing power of pensioners. They increase the other social benefits by EUR 800 million, with emphasis on family allowance, solidarity supplement for the elderly or social income of inclusion, supporting those who need the most.

In 2022, it began with the approval of the new status of the SNS and with the creation of the executive direction. In 2023, a vast modification of its structure was prepared, which will be implemented from January. The SNS will be organized throughout the country, in 39 local health units (ULS), which, with the citizen at the center of attention, group the responses of primary health care, hospitals and continuing care. The 39 ULS constitute a huge simplification of the current bureaucratic structure and replace, with a marked operational advantage, more than 100 bodies. at the same time, the State budget enshrines the recovery of the total autonomy of the SNS and its institutions. Born in Cantanhede, in 1951, Miguel Reis began his journey in the world of journalism, to which for more than a decade he devoted himself with enthusiasm and passion, having worked in the Newspaper, in the Luta, in Portugal Today, of which he was deputy director, and in the International RTP. Demonstrating the same commitment and sense of excellence that marked his journalistic career, he founded in 1992 a law firm that became a reference, not only in Portugal, but also internationally, with subsidiaries in countries such as Brazil, India and the United States. Specialist in Social Communication Law, he co-founded the Association of European Journalists, member of the Press Council and the High Authority for Social Communication, being an tireless defender of freedom of the press and justice. His life was guided by the promotion of impartial and free social communication. Faithful to all life to the roots and ideals of youth, he identified himself with democratic socialism and universalist humanism, symbolized in the divide “Freedom, Equality and Fraternity, ” which constituted for himself a civic inspiration and moral responsibility, not only leading him to want to be the voice of those who had no voice and to denounce unacceptable situations of injustice or illegality, but leading him to an action in the years that followed on 25 April, in which his voice and critical analysis were fundamental to understanding and reflection on Portuguese reality. Present and active in a historical period of consolidation of democratic institutions and search for new directions for collective life, Miguel Reis has always added a creative and transformative brand to the projects in which he worked, achieving prestige both in journalism and in law. Equipped with a great creative imagination, free and independent spirit, with strong political, cultural, literary and artistic interests, he was inconformist and irreverent in his attitudes, having been, in the civic level, a member of the Amadora Municipal Assembly, elected on the list of the Socialist Party, party of which he was militant. Thus, the Assembly of the Republic, meeting in plenary session, expresses its deep regret at the death of Miguel Reis, paying tribute to his memory and his example of citizen committed to the construction of a better country, presenting his condolences to the family, friends, colleagues, collaborators and all those who had the privilege of living and learning with Miguel Reis, recognizing his indelible legacy, both in journalism and in lawyers. ParlaMint-PT_2023-10-31.u1068	Mr President, the draft vote is of the following content: « Maria Margarida Carmo Tengarrinha died last October 26 at the age of 95. Born May 7, 1928, in Portimão, Margarida Tengarrinha, since his youth, participated in the student struggles of 1949 and 1954, in Lisbon, having been a member of the University Directorate of MUD Youth. He participated in the general exhibitions of plastic arts and was expelled from the School of Fine Arts of Lisbon ( ESBAL ) for the role played in the direction of the fight for peace at the NATO meeting in Lisbon in 1952. He joined the Portuguese Communist Party at the age of 24, in 1952, and went underground at the end of 1954. His first task was the creation, with his companion José Dias Coelho, of the production workshop of identification documents necessary for the clandestine intervention of the Party. In 1962, after the murder of José Dias Coelho, Margarida Tengarrinha went abroad, having performed tasks on Radio Portugal Livre. In 1968, he returned to Portugal, having taken on other partisan tasks in the underground. He participated in the World Congress of Women, held in Copenhagen in 1953, and in Moscow in 1963. Between 1974 and 1988, he belonged to the Central Committee of the CFP and was a Member of the Assembly of the Republic in the III and IV legislatures. The Portuguese Council's presidency for Peace and Cooperation was currently integrated. Margarida Tengarrinha left a vast work in the field of plastic arts and published several books on painting, popular culture and her experience in political intervention. The Assembly of the Republic, meeting in plenary, expresses its regret at the death of Margarida Tengarrinha and presents its condolences to its relatives and the Portuguese Communist Party. Do not keep on seeking. ”

He is the author of a vast scientific production, with emphasis on works in the fields of Labour Law, Obligations Law, Insurance Law and Introduction to the Law Study. It participated in several legislative work relating to labour legislation and the legal regime of insurance activity. In the 15th Constitutional Government, the request of the Ministry of Social Security and Labour, participated in the change to labour law, coordinating work on the revision of labour legislation that originated the Labour Code, between May 2002 and July 2003. In the XVII Constitutional Government, the request of the Ministry of Finance and Public Administration, participated in the review work of the Insurance Contract Scheme, coordinating the committee that prepared the draft of the legal regime of the insurance contract, Insurance Contract Law ( Decree-Law No 72 / 2008 of 16 April ). in a professional life of dedication to teaching, he taught several curricular units in the area of Civil Law, having stood out in Labour Law, and influenced, with his teaching, several generations of students. A professor of Law of Excellence, a civilist and juslaboralist of recognized scientific density, recognized by establishing bridges of dialogue and exemplary treatment, raised, with his contribution and in various ways, the prestige of the Faculty of Law of the University of Lisbon, leaving a scientific legacy for many generations of law students. He disappeared early. The Assembly of the Republic, meeting in plenary session, expresses its regret at the death of Pedro Romano Martinez, pays tribute to the teacher and expresses to the family, wife and children, the most sincere and sympathetic condolences. Do not keep on seeking. ”
